Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The linear_algebra variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 679 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 939 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the linear_algebra space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 679 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 939. QED.
